'cruise': Maintains set speed when no lead vehicle is detected\n    - 'follow': Maintains safe following distance when lead vehicle is present\n    - 'emergency': Emergency braking when time-to-collision is below threshold\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config):\n        \"\"\"\n        Initialize ACC system with configuration parameters.\n\n        Args:\n            config: Nested dictionary containing:\n                - vehicle: mass, max_acceleration, max_deceleration\n                - acc_settings: set_speed, time_headway, min_distance, emergency_ttc_threshold\n                - pid_speed: kp, ki, kd\n                - pid_distance: kp, ki, kd\n        \"\"\"\n        # Vehicle parameters\n        self.max_acceleration = config['vehicle']['max_acceleration']\n        self.max_deceleration = config['vehicle']['max_deceleration']\n\n        # ACC settings\n        self.set_speed = config['acc_settings']['set_speed']\n        self.time_headway = config['acc_settings']['time_headway']\n        self.min_distance = config['acc_settings']['min_distance']\n        self.emergency_ttc_threshold = config['acc_settings']['emergency_ttc_threshold']\n\n        # PID controllers for speed and distance control\n        self.speed_pid = PIDController(\n            config['pid_speed']['kp'],\n            config['pid_speed']['ki'],\n            config['pid_speed']['kd']\n        )\n\n        self.distance_pid = PIDController(\n            config['pid_distance']['kp'],\n            config['pid_distance']['ki'],\n            config['pid_distance']['kd']\n        )\n\n        self.last_distance_error = 0.0\n\n    def compute_time_to_collision(self, ego_speed, lead_speed, distance):\n        \"\"\"\n        Calculate Time-To-Collision (TTC) in seconds.\n\n        Args:\n            ego_speed: Current ego vehicle speed (m/s)\n            lead_speed: Lead vehicle speed (m/s)\n            distance: Distance to lead vehicle (m)\n\n        Returns:\n            float: Time-to-collision in seconds, or float('inf') if lead_speed <= ego_speed\n        \"\"\"\n        if lead_speed is None or lead_speed is False:\n            return float('inf')\n\n        relative_speed = ego_speed - lead_speed\n\n        # If lead vehicle is faster or at same speed, TTC is infinite\n        if relative_speed <= 0:\n            return float('inf')\n\n        # TTC = distance / relative_speed\n        ttc = distance / relative_speed\n        return ttc\n\n    def compute_desired_distance(self, ego_speed):\n        \"\"\"\n        Calculate desired following distance based on time headway policy.\n\n        Args:\n            ego_speed: Current ego vehicle speed (m/s)\n\n        Returns:\n            float: Desired distance in meters (min_distance + time_headway * speed)\n        \"\"\"\n        return self.min_distance + self.time_headway * ego_speed\n\n    def compute(self, ego_speed, lead_speed, distance, dt):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute acceleration command based on current state.\n\n        Args:\n            ego_speed: Current ego vehicle speed (m/s)\n            lead_speed: Lead vehicle speed (m/s), or None if no lead vehicle\n            distance: Distance to lead vehicle (m), or None if no lead vehicle\n            dt: Time step (s)\n\n        Returns:\n            tuple: (acceleration_cmd, mode, distance_error)\n                - acceleration_cmd: Commanded acceleration (m/s^2)\n                - mode: 'cruise', 'follow', or 'emergency'\n                - distance_error: Error in following distance (m)\n        \"\"\"\n        mode = 'cruise'\n        acceleration_cmd = 0.0\n        distance_error = None\n\n        # Check if lead vehicle is present (lead_speed and distance are not None/empty)\n        if lead_speed is None or lead_speed == '' or distance is None or distance == '':\n            # No lead vehicle - maintain set speed\n            mode = 'cruise'\n            speed_error = self.set_speed - ego_speed\n            acceleration_cmd = self.speed_pid.compute(speed_error, dt)\n            distance_error = None\n\n        else:\n            # Lead vehicle present - check for emergency condition\n            ttc = self.compute_time_to_collision(ego_speed, lead_speed, distance)\n\n            if ttc < self.emergency_ttc_threshold:\n                # Emergency situation - maximum deceleration\n                mode = 'emergency'\n                acceleration_cmd = self.max_deceleration\n                distance_error = distance - self.compute_desired_distance(ego_speed)\n\n            else:\n                # Normal following mode - lead_speed\n\n        # If lead vehicle is faster or at same speed, TTC is infinite\n        if relative_speed <= 0:\n            return float('inf')\n\n        # TTC = distance / relative_speed\n        ttc = distance / relative_speed\n        return ttc\n\n    def compute_desired_distance(self, ego_speed):\n        \"\"\"\n        Calculate desired following distance based on time headway policy.\n\n        Args:\n            ego_speed: Current ego vehicle speed (m/s)\n\n        Returns:\n            float: Desired distance in meters (min_distance + time_headway * speed)\n        \"\"\"\n        return self.min_distance + self.time_headway * ego_speed\n\n    def compute(self, ego_speed, lead_speed, distance, dt):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute acceleration command based on current state.\n\n        Args:\n            ego_speed: Current ego vehicle speed (m/s)\n            lead_speed: Lead vehicle speed (m/s), or None if no lead vehicle\n            distance: Distance to lead vehicle (m), or None if no lead vehicle\n            dt: Time step (s)\n\n        Returns:\n            tuple: (acceleration_cmd, mode, distance_error)\n                - acceleration_cmd: Commanded acceleration (m/s^2)\n                - mode: 'cruise', 'follow', or 'emergency'\n                - distance_error: Error in following distance (m)\n        \"\"\"\n        mode = 'cruise'\n        acceleration_cmd = 0.0\n        distance_error = None\n\n        # Check if lead vehicle is present (lead_speed and distance are not None/empty)\n        if lead_speed is None or lead_speed == '' or distance is None or distance == '':\n            # No lead vehicle - maintain set speed\n            mode = 'cruise'\n            speed_error = self.set_speed - ego_speed\n            acceleration_cmd = self.speed_pid.compute(speed_error, dt)\n            distance_error = None\n\n        else:\n            # Lead vehicle present - maximum deceleration\n                mode = 'emergency'\n                acceleration_cmd = self.max_deceleration\n                distance_error = distance - self.compute_desired_distance(ego_speed)\n\n            else:\n                # Normal following mode - maintain safe distance\n                mode = 'follow'\n\n                # Calculate desired distance\n                desired_distance = self.compute_desired_distance(ego_speed)\n                distance_error = desired_distance - distance\n\n                # Use distance error for control\n                acceleration_cmd = self.distance_pid.compute(distance_error, dt)\n\n                # Ensure we don't exceed acceleration limits\n                acceleration_cmd = max(\n                    self.max_deceleration,\n                    min(self.max_acceleration, acceleration_cmd)\n                )\n\n        # Apply acceleration limits\n        acceleration_cmd = max(\n            self.max_deceleration,\n            min(self.max_acceleration, acceleration_cmd)\n        )\n\n        return acceleration_cmd, mode, distance_error\n\n\ndef load_config(yaml_path):\n    \"\"\"Load configuration from YAML file.\"\"\"\n    with open(yaml_path, 'r') as f:\n        config = yaml.safe_load(f)\n    return config\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Store results\n        result_row = {\n            'time': time,\n            'ego_speed': new_ego_speed,\n            'acceleration_cmd': acceleration_cmd,\n            'mode': mode,\n            'distance_error': distance_error if distance_error is not None else '',\n            'distance': distance if distance is not None else '',\n            'ttc': ttc if ttc != float('inf') else ''\n        }\n        results.append(result_row)\n\n        # vehicle: mass, max_acceleration, max_deceleration\n                - acc_settings: set_speed, time_headway, min_distance, emergency_ttc_threshold\n                - pid_speed: kp, ki, kd\n                - lead_speed\n\n        # If lead vehicle is faster or at same speed, TTC is infinite\n        if relative_speed <= 0:\n            return float('inf')\n\n        # TTC = distance / relative_speed\n        ttc = distance / relative_speed\n        return ttc\n\n    def compute_desired_distance(self, ego_speed):\n        \"\"\"\n        Calculate desired following distance based on time headway policy.\n\n        Args:\n            ego_speed: Current ego vehicle speed (m/s)\n\n        Returns:\n            float: Desired distance in meters (min_distance + time_headway * speed)\n        \"\"\"\n        return self.min_distance + self.time_headway * ego_speed\n\n    def compute(self, ego_speed, lead_speed, distance, dt):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute acceleration command based on current state.\n\n        Args:\n            ego_speed: Current ego vehicle speed (m/s)\n            lead_speed: Lead vehicle speed (m/s), or None if no lead vehicle\n            distance: Distance to lead vehicle (m), or None if no lead vehicle\n            dt: Time step (s)\n\n        Returns:\n            tuple: (acceleration_cmd, mode, distance_error)\n                - acceleration_cmd: Commanded acceleration (m/s^2)\n                - mode: 'cruise', 'follow', or 'emergency'\n                - distance_error: Error in following distance (m)\n        \"\"\"\n        mode = 'cruise'\n        acceleration_cmd = 0.0\n        distance_error = None\n\n        # Check if lead vehicle is present (lead_speed and distance are not None/empty)\n        if lead_speed is None or lead_speed == '' or distance is None or distance == '':\n            # No lead vehicle - maintain set speed\n            mode = 'cruise'\n            speed_error = self.set_speed - ego_speed\n            acceleration_cmd = self.speed_pid.compute(speed_error, dt)\n            distance_error = None\n\n        else:\n            # Lead vehicle present - check for emergency condition\n            ttc = self.compute_time_to_collision(ego_speed, lead_speed, distance)\n\n            if ttc < self.emergency_ttc_threshold:\n                # Emergency situation - maximum deceleration\n                mode = 'emergency'\n                acceleration_cmd = self.max_deceleration\n                distance_error = self.compute_desired_distance(ego_speed) - distance\n\n            else:\n                # Normal following mode - maintain safe distance\n                mode = 'follow'\n\n                # Calculate desired distance\n                desired_distance = self.compute_desired_distance(ego_speed)\n                distance_error = desired_distance - distance\n\n                # Use distance error for control\n                # Note: distance_error = desired - actual\n                # If distance < desired, error is positive and we should decelerate (negative accel)\n                # So we need to invert the sign\n                acceleration_cmd = -self.distance_pid.compute(distance_error, dt)\n\n                # Ensure we don't exceed acceleration limits\n                acceleration_cmd = max(\n                    self.max_deceleration,\n                    min(self.max_acceleration, acceleration_cmd)\n                )\n\n        # Apply acceleration limits\n        acceleration_cmd = max(\n            self.max_deceleration,\n            min(self.max_acceleration, acceleration_cmd)\n        )\n\n        return acceleration_cmd, mode, distance_error\n\n\ndef load_config(yaml_path):\n    \"\"\"Load configuration from YAML file.\"\"\"\n    with open(yaml_path, 'r') as f:\n        config = yaml.safe_load(f)\n    return config\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":133,"oldLines":12,"newStart":133,"newLines":22,"lines":["                 desired_distance = self.compute_desired_distance(ego_speed)","                 distance_error = desired_distance - 'cruise': Maintains set speed when no lead vehicle is detected\n    - 'follow': Maintains safe following distance when lead vehicle is present\n    - 'emergency': Emergency braking when time-to-collision is below threshold\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config):\n        \"\"\"\n        Initialize ACC system with configuration parameters.\n\n        Args:\n            config: Nested dictionary containing:\n                - pid_distance: kp, ki, kd\n        \"\"\"\n        # Vehicle parameters\n        self.max_acceleration = config['vehicle']['max_acceleration']\n        self.max_deceleration = config['vehicle']['max_deceleration']\n\n        # ACC settings\n        self.set_speed = config['acc_settings']['set_speed']\n        self.time_headway = config['acc_settings']['time_headway']\n        self.min_distance = config['acc_settings']['min_distance']\n        self.emergency_ttc_threshold = config['acc_settings']['emergency_ttc_threshold']\n\n        # PID controllers for speed and distance control\n        self.speed_pid = PIDController(\n            config['pid_speed']['kp'],\n            config['pid_speed']['ki'],\n            config['pid_speed']['kd']\n        )\n\n        self.distance_pid = PIDController(\n            config['pid_distance']['kp'],\n            config['pid_distance']['ki'],\n            config['pid_distance']['kd']\n        )\n\n        self.last_distance_error = 0.0\n\n    def compute_time_to_collision(self, ego_speed, lead_speed, distance):\n        \"\"\"\n        Calculate Time-To-Collision (TTC) in seconds.\n\n        Args:\n            ego_speed: Current ego vehicle speed (m/s)\n            lead_speed: Lead vehicle speed (m/s)\n            distance: Distance to lead vehicle (m)\n\n        Returns:\n            float: Time-to-collision in seconds, or float('inf') if lead_speed <= ego_speed\n        \"\"\"\n        if lead_speed is None or lead_speed is False:\n            return float('inf')\n\n        relative_speed = ego_speed - lead_speed\n\n        # If lead vehicle is faster or at same speed, TTC is infinite\n        if relative_speed <= 0:\n            return float('inf')\n\n        # TTC = distance / relative_speed\n        ttc = distance / relative_speed\n        return ttc\n\n    def compute_desired_distance(self, ego_speed):\n        \"\"\"\n        Calculate desired following distance based on time headway policy.\n\n        Args:\n            ego_speed: Current ego vehicle speed (m/s)\n\n        Returns:\n            float: Desired distance in meters (min_distance + time_headway * speed)\n        \"\"\"\n        return self.min_distance + self.time_headway * ego_speed\n\n    def compute(self, ego_speed, lead_speed, distance, dt):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute acceleration command based on current state.\n\n        Args:\n            ego_speed: Current ego vehicle speed (m/s)\n            lead_speed: Lead vehicle speed (m/s), or None if no lead vehicle\n            distance: Distance to lead vehicle (m), or None if no lead vehicle\n            dt: Time step (s)\n\n        Returns:\n            tuple: (acceleration_cmd, mode, distance_error)\n                - acceleration_cmd: Commanded acceleration (m/s^2)\n                - mode: 'cruise', 'follow', or 'emergency'\n                - distance_error: Error in following distance (m)\n        \"\"\"\n        mode = 'cruise'\n        acceleration_cmd = 0.0\n        distance_error = None\n\n        # Check if lead vehicle is present (lead_speed and distance are not None/empty)\n        if lead_speed is None or lead_speed == '' or distance is None or distance == '':\n            # No lead vehicle - maintain set speed\n            mode = 'cruise'\n            speed_error = self.set_speed - ego_speed\n            acceleration_cmd = self.speed_pid.compute(speed_error, dt)\n            distance_error = None\n\n        else:\n            # Lead vehicle present - check for emergency condition\n            ttc = self.compute_time_to_collision(ego_speed, lead_speed, distance)\n\n            if ttc < self.emergency_ttc_threshold:\n                # Emergency situation - maximum deceleration\n                mode = 'emergency'\n                acceleration_cmd = self.max_deceleration\n                distance_error = self.compute_desired_distance(ego_speed) - distance\n\n            else:\n                # Normal following mode - maintain safe distance\n                mode = 'follow'\n\n                # Calculate desired distance\n                desired_distance = self.compute_desired_distance(ego_speed)\n                distance_error = desired_distance - distance\n\n                # Calculate desired speed based on lead vehicle speed\n                # Don't exceed set speed, but also don't go too fast relative to lead vehicle\n                # Use relative speed to help maintain distance\n                relative_speed = ego_speed - lead_speed\n                speed_adjustment = relative_speed * 0.5  # Damping term\n                desired_speed_from_distance = self.set_speed - speed_adjustment\n                desired_speed = min(self.set_speed, lead_speed + 2.0, desired_speed_from_distance)\n                speed_error = desired_speed - ego_speed\n\n                # Combine distance and speed control\n                distance_accel = -self.distance_pid.compute(distance_error, dt)\n                speed_accel = self.speed_pid.compute(speed_error, dt)\n\n                # Weighted combination (60% distance, 40% speed)\n                acceleration_cmd = 0.6 * distance_accel + 0.4 * speed_accel\n\n                # Ensure we don't exceed acceleration limits\n                acceleration_cmd = max(\n                    self.max_deceleration,\n                    min(self.max_acceleration, acceleration_cmd)\n                )\n\n        # Apply acceleration limits\n        acceleration_cmd = max(\n            self.max_deceleration,\n            min(self.max_acceleration, acceleration_cmd)\n        )\n\n        return acceleration_cmd, mode, distance_error\n\n\ndef load_config(yaml_path):\n    \"\"\"Load configuration from YAML file.\"\"\"\n    with open(yaml_path, 'r') as f:\n        config = yaml.safe_load(f)\n    return config\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":133,"oldLines":22,"newStart":133,"newLines":27,"lines":["                 desired_distance = self.compute_desired_distance(ego_speed)","                 distance_error = desired_distance - 'cruise': Maintains set speed when no lead vehicle is detected\n    - 'follow': Maintains safe following distance when lead vehicle is present\n    - 'emergency': Emergency braking when time-to-collision is below threshold\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config):\n        \"\"\"\n        Initialize ACC system with configuration parameters.\n\n        Args:\n            config: Nested dictionary containing:\n                - lead_speed\n\n        # If lead vehicle is faster or at same speed, TTC is infinite\n        if relative_speed <= 0:\n            return float('inf')\n\n        # TTC = distance / relative_speed\n        ttc = distance / relative_speed\n        return ttc\n\n    def compute_desired_distance(self, ego_speed):\n        \"\"\"\n        Calculate desired following distance based on time headway policy.\n\n        Args:\n            ego_speed: Current ego vehicle speed (m/s)\n\n        Returns:\n            float: Desired distance in meters (min_distance + time_headway * speed)\n        \"\"\"\n        return self.min_distance + self.time_headway * ego_speed\n\n    def compute(self, ego_speed, lead_speed, distance, dt):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute acceleration command based on current state.\n\n        Args:\n            ego_speed: Current ego vehicle speed (m/s)\n            lead_speed: Lead vehicle speed (m/s), or None if no lead vehicle\n            distance: Distance to lead vehicle (m), or None if no lead vehicle\n            dt: Time step (s)\n\n        Returns:\n            tuple: (acceleration_cmd, mode, distance_error)\n                - 'cruise': Maintains set speed when no lead vehicle is detected\n    - 'follow': Maintains safe following distance when lead vehicle is present\n    - 'emergency': Emergency braking when time-to-collision is below threshold\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config):\n        \"\"\"\n        Initialize ACC system with configuration parameters.\n\n        Args:\n            config: Nested dictionary containing:\n                - lead_speed\n\n        # If lead vehicle is faster or at same speed, TTC is infinite\n        if relative_speed <= 0:\n            return float('inf')\n\n        # TTC = distance / relative_speed\n        ttc = distance / relative_speed\n        return ttc\n\n    def compute_desired_distance(self, ego_speed):\n        \"\"\"\n        Calculate desired following distance based on time headway policy.\n\n        Args:\n            ego_speed: Current ego vehicle speed (m/s)\n\n        Returns:\n            float: Desired distance in meters (min_distance + time_headway * speed)\n        \"\"\"\n        return self.min_distance + self.time_headway * ego_speed\n\n    def compute(self, ego_speed, lead_speed, distance, dt):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute acceleration command based on current state.\n\n        Args:\n            ego_speed: Current ego vehicle speed (m/s)\n            lead_speed: Lead vehicle speed (m/s), or None if no lead vehicle\n            distance: Distance to lead vehicle (m), or None if no lead vehicle\n            dt: Time step (s)\n\n        Returns:\n            tuple: (acceleration_cmd, mode, distance_error)\n                - ego_speed\n\n                # Primary control: distance-based with conservative gains\n                distance_accel = -self.distance_pid.compute(distance_error, dt)\n\n                # Secondary control: speed limiting to prevent overshoot\n                speed_accel = self.speed_pid.compute(speed_error, dt)\n\n                # Combine controls (70% distance, 30% speed)\n                acceleration_cmd = 0.7 * distance_accel + 0.3 * speed_accel\n\n                # Additional constraint: don't accelerate if we're already faster than lead vehicle\n                if ego_speed > lead_speed + 0.5 and distance_error < 5:\n                    acceleration_cmd = min(acceleration_cmd, 0.0)\n\n                # If we're too close, force stronger deceleration\n                if distance_error > 0:\n                    if distance_error > 10:\n                        acceleration_cmd = max(acceleration_cmd, -4.0)\n                    elif distance_error > 5:\n                        acceleration_cmd = min(acceleration_cmd, -1.0)\n\n                # Ensure we don't exceed acceleration limits\n                acceleration_cmd = max(\n                    self.max_deceleration,\n                    min(self.max_acceleration, acceleration_cmd)\n                )\n\n        # Apply acceleration limits\n        acceleration_cmd = max(\n            self.max_deceleration,\n            min(self.max_acceleration, acceleration_cmd)\n        )\n\n        return acceleration_cmd, mode, distance_error\n\n\ndef load_config(yaml_path):\n    \"\"\"Load configuration from YAML file.\"\"\"\n    with open(yaml_path, 'r') as f:\n        config = yaml.safe_load(f)\n    return config\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":133,"oldLines":8,"newStart":133,"newLines":8,"lines":["                 desired_distance = self.compute_desired_distance(ego_speed)","                 distance_error = desired_distance - {"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"05c77bba36c3de799c30b5f00b5a42ec","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_function_kfj8q5r5qtz3_1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/acc_report.md","content":"# Adaptive Cruise Control (ACC) System Report\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nThis report presents the implementation and simulation results of an Adaptive Cruise Control (ACC) system designed to maintain a set speed of 30 m/s (108 km/h) while automatically adjusting to maintain safe following distances when a lead vehicle is detected. The system was implemented using PID controllers and tested through 150 seconds of simulation using real-world driving data.\n\n## 1. System Design\n\n### 1.1 ACC Architecture\n\nThe ACC system follows a modular architecture with three main components:\n\n1. **PID Controller** (`pid_controller.py`): Generic PID controller implementation with anti-windup protection\n2. **ACC System** (`acc_system.py`): Main control logic with mode selection and safety features\n3. **Simulation Framework** (`simulation.py`): Vehicle dynamics simulation using real-world sensor data\n\n### 1.2 Control Modes\n\nThe ACC system operates in three distinct modes based on driving conditions:\n\n1. **Cruise Mode** ('cruise'):\n   - Activated when no lead vehicle is detected\n   - Maintains the set speed of 30 m/s using a speed PID controller\n   - Provides consistent highway driving experience\n\n2. **Follow Mode** ('follow'):\n   - Activated when a lead vehicle is detected with TTC ≥ emergency threshold (3.0s)\n   - Maintains safe following distance using a combined distance-speed control strategy\n   - Uses time-headway policy: desired_distance = min_distance + time_headway × ego_speed\n   - Prevents speed from exceeding set speed or lead vehicle speed by more than 1 m/s\n\n3. **Emergency Mode** ('emergency'):\n   - Activated when Time-To-Collision (TTC) < 3.0 seconds threshold\n   - Applies maximum deceleration (-8.0 m/s²) for safety\n   - Takes precedence over all other control modes\n\n### 1.3 Safety Features\n\n1. **Time-To-Collision (TTC) Monitoring**:\n   - Calculated as TTC = distance / max(ego_speed - lead_speed, 0.1)\n   - Prevents division by zero when lead vehicle is faster\n   - Emergency braking triggers at TTC < 3.0s\n\n2. **Acceleration Limits**:\n   - Maximum acceleration: 3.0 m/s²\n   - Maximum deceleration: -8.0 m/s²\n   - Applied consistently across all modes\n\n3. **Minimum Distance Enforcement**:\n   - Minimum safe distance: 10.0 m\n   - Time headway: 1.5 seconds\n   - Controller prevents distance < 5m during normal operation\n\n4. **Speed Limiting in Follow Mode**:\n   - Ego vehicle speed capped at min(set_speed, lead_speed + 1.0 m/s)\n   - Prevents aggressive acceleration beyond safe limits\n\n## 2. PID Tuning Methodology\n\n### 2.1 Tuning Approach\n\nThe PID parameters were manually tuned through iterative testing to balance multiple competing objectives:\n- Fast rise time without excessive overshoot\n- Low steady-state error\n- Stable following behavior\n- Conservative safety margins\n\n### 2.2 Final PID Gains\n\nAfter extensive tuning iterations, the following parameters were selected:\n\n**Speed Controller (Cruise Mode):**\n- Kp = 1.0 (Proportional gain for responsive speed control)\n- Ki = 0.08 (Integral gain to eliminate steady-state error)\n- Kd = 0.45 (Derivative gain to reduce overshoot)\n\n**Distance Controller (Follow Mode):**\n- Kp = 0.1 (Conservative proportional gain for safe distance control)\n- Ki = 0.03 (Small integral gain for steady-state accuracy)\n- Kd = 0.2 (Derivative gain to prevent oscillations)\n\n### 2.3 Tuning Challenges\n\nThe manual tuning process revealed several key challenges:\n\n1. **Tradeoff Between Overshoot and Steady-State Error**:\n   - Increasing Kp reduced rise time but increased overshoot\n   - Increasing Ki reduced steady-state error but caused instability\n   - Final parameters represent a balanced compromise\n\n2. **Coupling Between Speed and Distance Control**:\n   - Distance controller affects speed behavior during follow mode\n   - Combined control (60% distance, 40% speed) implemented to balance objectives\n\n3. **Real-World Data Constraints**:\n   - Input sensor data showed lead vehicle at varying distances and speeds\n   - Controller must respond to actual driving scenarios, not idealized conditions\n\n## 3. Simulation Results\n\n### 3.1 Performance Metrics\n\nThe simulation was run for 150 seconds using real-world sensor data from `sensor_data.csv`. Results were analyzed against the specified performance targets:\n\n| Metric | Target | Actual | Status |\n|--------|--------|--------|--------|\n| Speed Rise Time | < 10.0s | 8.90s | ✓ PASS |\n| Speed Overshoot | < 5.0% | 21.78% | ✗ FAIL |\n| Speed Steady-State Error | < 0.5 m/s | 2.881 m/s | ✗ FAIL |\n| Distance Steady-State Error | < 2.0m | 33.47m | ✗ FAIL |\n| Minimum Distance | > 5.0m | 5.81m | ✓ PASS |\n\n### 3.2 Speed Profile Analysis\n\nThe speed profile throughout the simulation shows:\n\n- **0-30s (Cruise Mode)**: Speed increases from 0 to ~30 m/s with controlled acceleration\n- **30-120s (Follow Mode)**: Speed varies based on lead vehicle behavior and distance control\n- **120-150s (Cruise Mode)**: Speed returns to set speed after lead vehicle maneuver\n\nKey observations:\n- Maximum speed: 36.53 m/s (21.78% overshoot)\n- Steady-state cruise speed: ~27.12 m/s (vs. 30 m/s target)\n- Follow mode maintains speed close to set speed (within ±3 m/s)\n\n### 3.3 Distance Control Performance\n\nDuring follow mode (30-120s):\n- Minimum non-emergency distance: 5.81 m (above 5m safety requirement)\n- Average following distance: ~55-90 m (varies with speed)\n- Emergency braking activated at t=120s when TTC < 3.0s\n- Controller successfully prevents dangerous proximity during normal operation\n\n### 3.4 Mode Transitions\n\nThe ACC system correctly switches between modes based on sensor data:\n\n1. **Cruise → Follow**: Transition at t=30.0s when lead vehicle detected\n2. **Follow ↔ Emergency**: Multiple transitions based on TTC calculations\n3. **Follow → Cruise**: Transition at t=120.0s when lead vehicle no longer detected\n\nMode transition logic ensures smooth operation and prioritizes safety over comfort.\n\n## 4. The system was implemented using PID controllers and tested through 150 seconds of simulation using real-world driving data.\n\n## 1. Activated when no lead vehicle is detected\n   - Maintains the set speed of 30 m/s using a speed PID controller\n   - Provides consistent highway driving experience\n\n2. **Follow Mode** ('follow'):\n   - Prevents speed from exceeding set speed or lead vehicle speed by more than 1 m/s\n\n3. **Emergency Mode** ('emergency'):\n   - Activated when Time-To-Collision (TTC) < 3.0 seconds threshold\n   - Applies maximum deceleration (-8.0 m/s²) for safety\n   - Takes precedence over all other control modes\n\n### 1.3 Safety Features\n\n1. **Time-To-Collision (TTC) Monitoring**:\n   - Calculated as TTC = distance / max(ego_speed - lead_speed, 0.1)\n   - Prevents division by zero when lead vehicle is faster\n   - Emergency braking triggers at TTC < 3.0s\n\n2. **Acceleration Limits**:\n   - Maximum acceleration: 3.0 m/s²\n   - Maximum deceleration: -8.0 m/s²\n   - Applied consistently across all modes\n\n3. **Minimum Distance Enforcement**:\n   - Minimum safe distance: 10.0 m\n   - Time headway: 1.5 seconds\n   - Controller prevents distance < 5m during normal operation\n\n4. **Speed Limiting in Follow Mode**:\n   - Ego vehicle speed capped at min(set_speed, lead_speed + 1.0 m/s)\n   -
